:swe-json-mediatype: application/swe+json


[[clause-encoding-swejson]]
=== Requirements Class "SWE Common JSON Encoding"
[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
type:: class
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json
subject:: Web API
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/req/json-encoding-rules++[]

requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-read
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-write
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/observation-encoding
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/cmdschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/cmdschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-json/command-encoding
====


==== Overview

This requirements class defines JSON encodings for pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources based on <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.


==== Media Type

NOTE: Implementations should use *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`application/vnd.ogc.swe+json`]* as a preliminary media type until the <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>> Standard is stable to avoid confusing future implementations accessing JSON documents from draft versions of the Standard. The media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] will be registered for SWE Common JSON encoding, if and once this Standard is approved by the OGC Members. This note will be removed before publishing this Standard.

The media type used when using the SWE Common JSON encoding is *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`]*.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-read

part:: The server SHALL accept resource retrieval (read) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The response to such request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-write

conditions:: The server implements <<clause-resource-crd,style=basic%>>.

part:: The server SHALL accept resource insertion (write) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The resource representation provided in the request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====



[[clause-swejson-obsschema]]
==== Observation Schema Representation

The observation schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-schema

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation Schema`] resource for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] SHALL be valid against the JSON schema {json-observationschema-schema-swe}.

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`encoding`] property SHALL be set to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`JSONEncoding`] object.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping

[.component,class=part]
--
The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property SHALL include at least one pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Time`] component corresponding to either pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resultTime`] or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`phenomenonTime`]. This pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Time`] component SHALL be identified using one of the following URIs as the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`definition`] property.

For pass:c,q,a,m,p[`phenomenonTime`]: +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/phenomenonTime`], or +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.opengis.net/def/property/OGC/0/SamplingTime`]

For pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resultTime`]: +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/resultTime`]
--

[.component,class=part]
--
If the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property includes a reference to a sampling feature, a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Text`] component SHALL be used.

The component SHALL be identified using the following URI as the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`definition`] property: +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/FeatureOfInterest`]

The value of the component SHALL be the local identifier of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`SamplingFeature`] resource.
--
====



[[clause-swejson-observation]]
==== Observation Representation

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/observation-encoding

part:: pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources SHALL be encoded according to the schema provided by the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`], using the encoding rules defined in link:++https://docs.ogc.org/DRAFTS/24-014.html#encoding_rules_json++[Clause 10.2: Requirements Class: JSON Encoding Rules] of <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.
====



[[clause-swejson-comdschema]]
==== Command Schema Representation

The command schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/cmdschema-schema

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command Schema`] resource for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] SHALL be valid against the JSON schema {json-commandschema-schema-swe}.

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`encoding`] property SHALL be set to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`JSONEncoding`] object.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/cmdschema-mapping

[.component,class=part]
--
If the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property includes a timestamp to be mapped to the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`issueTime`] property of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`CommandResource`], a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Time`] component SHALL be used.

The component SHALL be identified using the following URI as the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`definition`] property: +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.opengis.net/def/property/OGC/0/IssueTime`]
--

[.component,class=part]
--
If the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property includes a reference to a sampling feature, a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Text`] component SHALL be used.

The component SHALL be identified using the following URI as the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`definition`] property: +
pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/FeatureOfInterest`]

The value of the component SHALL be the local identifier of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`SamplingFeature`] resource.
--
====



[[clause-swejson-command]]
==== Command Representation

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-json/command-encoding

part:: pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources SHALL be encoded according to the schema provided by the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`], using the encoding rules defined in link:++https://docs.ogc.org/DRAFTS/24-014.html#encoding_rules_json++[Clause 10.2: Requirements Class: JSON Encoding Rules] of <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.
====

:swe-text-mediatype: application/swe+text


[[clause-encoding-swetext]]
=== Requirements Class "SWE Common Text Encoding"
[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
type:: class
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text
subject:: Web API
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/req/text-encoding-rules++[]

requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-read
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-write
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/obsschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/obsschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/observation-encoding
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/cmdschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/cmdschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-text/command-encoding
====


==== Overview

This requirements class defines text encodings (delimiter separated values, or DSV) for pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources based on the Text Encoding defined in the <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>> Standard.


==== Media Type

NOTE: Implementations should use *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`application/vnd.ogc.swe+text`]* as a preliminary media type until the <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>> Standard is stable to avoid confusing future implementations accessing JSON documents from draft versions of the Standard. The media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] will be registered for SWE Common Text encoding, if and once this Standard is approved by the OGC Members. This note will be removed before publishing this Standard.

The media type used when using the SWE Common Text encoding is *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`]*.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-read

part:: The server SHALL accept resource retrieval (read) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The response to such request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-write

conditions:: The server implements <<clause-resource-crd,style=basic%>>.

part:: The server SHALL accept resource insertion (write) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The resource representation provided in the request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====



[[clause-swetext-obsschema]]
==== Observation Schema Representation

The observation schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/obsschema-schema

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation Schema`] resource for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] SHALL be valid against the JSON schema {json-observationschema-schema-swe}.

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`encoding`] property SHALL be set to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`TextEncoding`] object.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/obsschema-mapping

The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property SHALL fulfill xref:/req/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping[].
====



[[clause-swetext-observation]]
==== Observation Representation

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/observation-encoding

part:: pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources SHALL be encoded according to the schema provided by the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`], using the encoding rules defined in link:++https://docs.ogc.org/DRAFTS/24-014.html#encoding_rules_text++[Clause 10.3: Requirements Class: Text Encoding Rules] of <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.
====



[[clause-swetext-comdschema]]
==== Command Schema Representation

The command schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/cmdschema-schema

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command Schema`] resource for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] SHALL be valid against the JSON schema {json-commandschema-schema-swe}.

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`encoding`] property SHALL be set to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`TextEncoding`] object.
====

====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/cmdschema-mapping

The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`recordSchema`] property SHALL fulfill xref:/req/swecommon-json/cmdschema-mapping[].

[[clause-swetext-command]]
==== Command Representation

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-text/command-encoding

part:: pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources SHALL be encoded according to the schema provided by the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`], using the encoding rules defined in link:++https://docs.ogc.org/DRAFTS/24-014.html#encoding_rules_text++[Clause 10.3: Requirements Class: Text Encoding Rules] of <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.
====




:swe-binary-mediatype: application/swe+binary


[[clause-encoding-swebinary]]
=== Requirements Class "SWE Common Binary Encoding"
[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
type:: class
identifier:: /req/swecommon-binary
subject:: Web API
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/req/binary-encoding-rules++[]

requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/mediatype-read
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/mediatype-write
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/obsschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/obsschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/observation-encoding
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/cmdschema-schema
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/cmdschema-mapping
requirement:: /req/swecommon-binary/command-encoding
====


==== Overview

This requirements class defines binary encodings of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources based on the Binary Encoding defined in the <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>> Standard.

The main objective of this encoding is better data size efficiency than text or JSON, thus allowing for:

- Transfer of observations and commands over very low power / low bandwidth network links (e.g., LoRa,  etc.); and
- Transfer high bandwidth data sets such as raster data (e.g., video, LiDAR, etc.).

For even better efficiency, this encoding can be combined with a transport protocol such as <<clause-protocol-mqtt,MQTT>>.


==== Media Type

NOTE: Implementations should use *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`application/vnd.ogc.swe+binary`]* as a preliminary media type until the <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>> Standard is stable to avoid confusing future implementations accessing JSON documents from draft versions of the Standard. The media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`] will be registered for SWE Common binary encoding, if and once this Standard is approved by the OGC Members. This note will be removed before publishing this Standard.

The media type used when using the SWE Common Binary encoding is *pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`]*.

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-binary/mediatype-read

part:: The server SHALL accept resource retrieval (read) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The response to such request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-binary/mediatype-write

conditions:: The server implements <<clause-resource-crd,style=basic%>>.

part:: The server SHALL accept resource insertion (write) requests with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`] for all resource types whose representation is specified in this requirements class.

part:: The resource representation provided in the request SHALL be encoded as specified in the clause corresponding to the resource type.
====



[[clause-swebinary-obsschema]]

The observation schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[requirement,model=ogc]

part:: The pass:c,q,a,m,p[`encoding`] property SHALL be set to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`BinaryEncoding`] object.

====



[[clause-swebinary-observation]]

====



[[clause-swebinary-comdschema]]
==== Command Schema Representation

The command schema for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-binary-mediatype}`] media type is a SWE Common data component tree provided in JSON format.

[[clause-swebinary-command]]

[requirement,model=ogc]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /req/swecommon-binary/command-encoding

part:: pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources SHALL be encoded according to the schema provided by the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`], using the encoding rules defined in link:++https://docs.ogc.org/DRAFTS/24-014.html#encoding_rules_text++[Clause 10.4: Requirements Class: Binary Encoding Rules] of <<OGC-SWECOMMON,SWE Common 3.0>>.

====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/api-common/resource-collection
target:: /req/api-common/resource-collection

test-purpose:: Verify that resource collections are implemented like feature collections.

test-method::
step::: For each resource collection available on the server with the property pass:c,q,a,m,p[`itemType`] that is NOT set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`feature`]:
step:::: Execute the following tests from {ogcapi-features-1}: +
         - /conf/core/fc-md-links
         - /conf/core/fc-md-items
         - /conf/core/fc-md-items-links
         - /conf/core/fc-md-extent
         - /conf/core/sfc-md-op
         - /conf/core/sfc-md-success
         - /conf/core/fc-op
         - /conf/core/fc-limit-definition
         - /conf/core/fc-limit-response
         - /conf/core/query-param-invalid
         - /conf/core/query-param-unknown
         - /conf/core/fc-links
         - /conf/core/fc-timeStamp
         - /conf/core/fc-numberMatched
         - /conf/core/fc-numberReturned
         - /conf/core/f-op
         - /conf/core/f-success
         - /conf/core/f-links
====

// Datastreams

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/sf-ref-from-datastream
target:: /req/datastream/sf-ref-from-datastream

test-purpose:: Validate that pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Sampling Features`] associated to a given datastream are available as sub-resources.

test-method::
step::: Retrieve all pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources by executing test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/canonical-resources with parameter pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resource-type=datastreams`].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Validate that the server implements a Sampling Feature resources endpoint at path pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/samplingFeatures`] using test {part1-specurl}/conf/sf/resources-endpoint, where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`dsId`] is the local ID of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/foi-ref-from-datastream
target:: /req/datastream/foi-ref-from-datastream

test-purpose:: Validate that pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Features of Interest`] associated to a given datastream are available as sub-resources.

test-method::
step::: Retrieve all pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources by executing test {part1-spec}/conf/api-common/canonical-resources with parameter pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resource-type=datastreams`].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at path pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/featuresOfInterest`], where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`dsId`] is the local ID of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Iterate through the list of resources in the response, following pass:c,q,a,m,p[`next`] links as appropriate.
step:::: If the response content type is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{geojson-mediatype}`], validate the response using the GeoJSON schema.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/canonical-url
target:: /req/datastream/canonical-url

test-purpose:: Validate that every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource is accessible via its canonical URL.

test-method:: For every collection advertised by the server with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`itemType`] property set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`]:
step::: Retrieve the collection items as described in test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/collection-items.
step::: For each item, check that a link with relation type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`canonical`] is included.
step::: Dereference this link and validate that a document is returned with a status code 200.
step::: Check that the returned document has the same content as the resource originally included in the collection items (except for the canonical link).
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/resources-endpoint
target:: /req/datastream/resources-endpoint

test-purpose:: Validate that the server implements a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources endpoint correctly. +
_This is a parameterized test that requires the endpoint URL as a parameter_

test-method::
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request to the endpoint URL.
step::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step::: Validate that the contents of the returned document conform to the media type reported by the response pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header.
step:::: If the response content type is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`], execute test xref:/conf/json/datastream-schema[style=id%].
step:::: For other response content types not supported by the testing engine, issue a warning and skip this test.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/canonical-endpoint
target:: /req/datastream/canonical-endpoint

test-purpose:: Validate that the server exposes the canonical pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources endpoint.

test-method:: Validate that the server implements a {datastream-resources-endpoint} at path pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams`] using test xref:/conf/datastream/resources-endpoint[style=id%].
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/ref-from-system
target:: /req/datastream/ref-from-system

test-purpose:: Validate that pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources associated to a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`System`] are available as sub-resources.

test-method::
step::: Retrieve all pass:c,q,a,m,p[`System`] resources by executing test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/canonical-resources with parameter pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resource-type=systems`].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`System`] resource in the response:
step:::: Validate that the server implements a {datastream-resources-endpoint} at path pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/systems/{sysId}/datastreams`] using test xref:/conf/datastream/resources-endpoint[style=id%], where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`sysId`] is the local ID of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`System`] resource.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/ref-from-deployment
target:: /req/datastream/ref-from-deployment

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/collections
target:: /req/datastream/collections

test-purpose:: Validate that pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] collections are tagged with the proper item type.

test-method:: For every collection advertised by the server with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`itemType`] property set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`]:
step::: Retrieve the collection items as described in test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/collection-items.
step::: Validate that the contents of the returned document conform to the media type reported by the response pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header.

// Observation Schema

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/datastream/schema-op
target:: /req/datastream/schema-op

test-purpose:: Validate that every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource has a schema sub-resource.

test-method::
step::: Retrieve all pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources by executing test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/canonical-resources with parameter pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resource-type=datastreams`].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Retrieve the list of supported observation formats listed in the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
step:::: For each supported observation format, issue an HTTP GET request at path pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/schema?obsFormat={format}`], where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`dsId`] is the local ID of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource and pass:c,q,a,m,p[`format`] is the supported format, and validate that a document is returned with a status code 200.
====

[abstract_test]

test-purpose:: Validate that the server implements a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`SystemEvent`] resources endpoint correctly. +
_This is a parameterized test that requires the endpoint URL as a parameter_

test-method::
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request to the endpoint URL.
step::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step::: Validate that the contents of the returned document conform to the media type reported by the response pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header.
step:::: If the response content type is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`], execute test xref:/conf/json/systemevent-schema[style=id%].
step:::: For other response content types not supported by the testing engine, issue a warning and skip this test.
====

step::: Validate the response using the steps described in test xref:/conf/datastream/resources-endpoint[style=id%].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Retrieve its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`phenomenonTime`] property.
step:::: Verify that the value of the property intersects the time specified in the request.

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/advanced-filtering/datastream-by-obsprop
target:: /req/advanced-filtering/datastream-by-obsprop

test-purpose:: Validate that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`observedProperty`] query parameter is processed correctly.

test-method::
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams?observedProperty={idList}`] where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`] is a list of one or more local IDs of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Property`] resources. +
See test {part1-specurl}/conf/advanced-filtering/id-list-schema
step::: Validate the response using the steps described in test xref:/conf/datastream/resources-endpoint[style=id%].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Retrieve all observed properties listed in the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
step:::: Verify that at least one of the collected properties has one of the identifiers included in pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`].
step::: Repeat the previous steps with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`observedProperty`] parameter set to a list of one or more URIs identifying observable properties.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/advanced-filtering/datastream-by-foi
target:: /req/advanced-filtering/datastream-by-foi

test-purpose:: Validate that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`foi`] query parameter is processed correctly.

test-method::
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams?foi={idList}`] where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`] is a list of one or more local IDs of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Sampling Feature`] or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Feature`] resources. +
See test {part1-specurl}/conf/advanced-filtering/id-list-schema
step::: Validate the response using the steps described in test xref:/conf/datastream/resources-endpoint[style=id%].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource in the response:
step:::: Retrieve the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`]'s sampling features by issuing an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/samplingFeatures`].
step:::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Sampling Feature`] resource in the returned collection:
         . Follow the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`sampleOf`] links to retrieve the target features, recursively. If a link does not resolve or the link media type is not supported by the testing engine, use the link target as the identifier of the feature.
step:::: Verify that at least one of the collected features has one of the identifiers included in pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`].
step::: Repeat the previous steps with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`foi`] parameter set to a list of one or more UIDs of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Feature`] resources.
====

step:::: Follow the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`sampleOf`] links to retrieve the sampled features, recursively. If a link does not resolve or the link media type is not supported by the testing engine, use the link target as the identifier of the feature.
step:::: Verify that at least one of the collected features has one of the identifiers included in pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`].
step::: Repeat the previous steps with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`foi`] parameter set to a list of one or more UIDs of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Feature`] resources.

step::: Repeat the steps above for every Command resources endpoint nested under a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`] resource, that is at endpoints pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/controlstreams/{csId}/commands`] where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`csId`] is the local ID of a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`] resource.
====

// CommandStatus

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/advanced-filtering/status-by-statuscode
target:: /req/advanced-filtering/status-by-statuscode

test-purpose:: Validate that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`statusCode`] query parameter is processed correctly.

test-method:: Retrieve all pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources by executing test {part1-specurl}/conf/api-common/canonical-resources with parameter pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resource-type=commands`], then for or every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resource:
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/commands/{cmdId}/status?statusCode={idList}`] where pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{idList}`] is a list of one or more status codes (see requirement for the possible values).
step::: Validate the response using the steps described in test xref:/conf/controlstream/status-resources-endpoint[style=id%].
step::: For each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resource in the response:
step:::: Retrieve its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`currentStatus`] property
step:::: Verify that the value of the property is equal to one of the status codes listed in the request.
====

// DataStreams

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/create-replace-delete/datastream
target:: /req/create-replace-delete/datastream

test-purpose:: Validate that the server implements CREATE/REPLACE/DELETE operations correctly for pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources.

test-method::
step::: Validate each pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] representation submitted in a CREATE or REPLACE request against the write schema {json-datastream-create-schema}.
step::: Execute all tests from conformance class {ogcapi-features-4-spec-url}/conf/create-replace-delete at the following endpoints:
step:::: At resources endpoint pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/systems/{sysId}/datastreams`] (for CREATE)
step:::: At resource endpoint pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/systems/{sysId}/datastreams/{id}`] (for REPLACE and DELETE)
step:::: At resource endpoint pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{id}`] (for REPLACE and DELETE)
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/create-replace-delete/datastream-live
target:: /req/create-replace-delete/datastream-live

test-purpose:: Validate that a client controls pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] liveness and that disabled datastreams reject new observations without hiding existing resources.

test-method::
step::: Create a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource with a valid representation whose pass:c,q,a,m,p[`live`] property is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`false`].
step::: Retrieve the created resource and verify that its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`live`] property is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`false`].
step::: Submit a valid pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] CREATE request at the created resource's pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/observations`] endpoint and verify that the response has status code 409.
step::: REPLACE the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource with the same valid representation except that its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`live`] property is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`true`].
step::: Retrieve the resource and verify that its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`live`] property is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`true`].
step::: Submit a valid pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] CREATE request and verify that it succeeds.
step::: REPLACE the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource with the same valid representation except that its pass:c,q,a,m,p[`live`] property is pass:c,q,a,m,p[`false`].
step::: Retrieve the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource and the Observation created in the preceding step, and verify that both remain available.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/create-replace-delete/datastream-update-schema
target:: /req/create-replace-delete/datastream-update-schema

test-purpose:: Validate that the server rejects pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] REPLACE requests with incompatible schemas.

test-method::
step::: Given a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource with ID pass:c,q,a,m,p[`dsId`] that has associated observations:
step:::: Issue an HTTP PUT request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}`] with a different observation schema.
step:::: Verify that the server responds with an error code 409.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/create-replace-delete/datastream-delete-cascade
target:: /req/create-replace-delete/datastream-delete-cascade

test-purpose:: Validate that the server implements the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`cascade`] query parameter correctly.

test-method::
step::: Given a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource with ID pass:c,q,a,m,p[`dsId`] that has observations:
step:::: Issue an HTTP DELETE request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}?cascade=false`].
step:::: Verify that the server responds with an error code 409.
step:::: Issue an HTTP DELETE request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}?cascade=true`].
step:::: Verify that the datastream and all its observations have been deleted.
====

test-purpose:: Validate that the server rejects pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`] UPDATE requests with incompatible schemas.

test-method::
step::: Given a pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`] resource with ID pass:c,q,a,m,p[`csId`] that has associated commands:
step:::: Issue an HTTP UPDATE request at URL pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/controlstreams/{csId}`] with a different command schema.
step:::: Verify that the server responds with an error code 409.
====

=== Conformance Class "JSON Encoding"

[conformance_class]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json
target:: /req/json
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/conf/json-record-components++[]
classification:: Target Type:Web API

conformance-test:: /conf/json/mediatype-read
conformance-test:: /conf/json/mediatype-write
conformance-test:: /conf/json/datastream-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/obsschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/observation-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/observation-constraints
conformance-test:: /conf/json/controlstream-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/commandschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/command-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/command-constraints
conformance-test:: /conf/json/commandstatus-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/commandresult-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/json/commandresult-constraints
conformance-test:: /conf/json/systemevent-schema
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json/mediatype-read
target:: /req/json/mediatype-read

test-purpose:: Verify that the server supports the JSON format on retrieval operations.

test-method::
step::: For each supported conformance class:
step:::: Request resources from the specified resources endpoint with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Verify that the server responds with HTTP code 200.
step:::: Verify that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header of the response is set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Verify that the response is properly encoded as JSON.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json/mediatype-write
target:: /req/json/mediatype-write

test-purpose:: Verify that the server advertises support for the JSON format on transactional operations.

test-method::
step::: For each supported conformance class:
step:::: Verify that server advertises support for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`] in the API definition for CREATE or REPLACE operations, for the specified resources endpoint.
====

// DataStreams

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json/datastream-schema
target:: /req/json/datastream-schema

test-purpose:: Validate that the JSON representation of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources is valid.

test-method::
step::: Request a single pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{id}`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the document against the schema {json-datastream-schema} using a JSON Schema validator.

step::: Request multiple pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources.
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the document against the schema {json-datastream-collection-schema} using a JSON Schema validator.
step:::: Repeat the steps above for nested pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resources endpoints pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/systems/{sysId}/datastreams`].
====

step::: Validate the document against the schema {json-observationschema-schema-json} using a JSON Schema validator.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json/observation-schema
target:: /req/json/observation-schema

test-purpose:: Validate that the JSON representation of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources is valid.

test-method::
step::: Request a single pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resource.
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/observations/{id}`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the document against the schema {json-observation-schema} using a JSON Schema validator.

step::: Request multiple pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources.
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/observations`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the document against the schema {json-observation-collection-schema} using a JSON Schema validator.
step:::: For each observation in the response, validate it with test xref:/conf/json/observation-constraints[style=id%]
step:::: Repeat the steps above for nested pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources endpoints pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/observations`].
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/json/observation-constraints
target:: /req/json/observation-constraints

test-purpose:: Validate that pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] result and parameters are encoded properly.

test-method::
step::: Retrieve the schema from the parent pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource.
step::: Validate that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] result is valid according to the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`resultSchema`].
step::: Validate that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] parameters, if any, are valid according to the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`parametersSchema`].
====

=== Conformance Class "SWE Common JSON Encoding"

[conformance_class]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json
target:: /req/swecommon-json
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/conf/json-encoding-rules++[]
classification:: Target Type:Web API

conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/mediatype-read
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/mediatype-write
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/obsschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/observation-encoding
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/cmdschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-json/cmdschema-mapping

// Media Types

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json/mediatype-read
target:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-read

test-purpose:: Verify that the server supports the SWE Common JSON format on retrieval operations.

test-method::
step::: For at least one of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and/or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources endpoint:
step:::: Verify that the server advertises support for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] in the API definition for retrieval operations.
step:::: Request resources from the resources endpoint with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Verify that the server responds with HTTP code 200.
step:::: Verify that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header of the response is set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Verify that the response is properly encoded as JSON.
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json/mediatype-write
target:: /req/swecommon-json/mediatype-write

test-purpose:: Verify that the server advertises support for the SWE Common JSON format on transactional operations.

test-method::
step::: For at least one of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and/or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources endpoint:
step:::: Verify that the server advertises support for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] in the API definition for CREATE or REPLACE operations.
====

// Observations

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json/obsschema-schema
target:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-schema

test-purpose:: Validate that the JSON representation of observation schema resources is valid.

test-method:: For every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] resource:
step::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{id}/schema?obsFormat={swe-json-mediatype}`].
step::: Validate the document against the schema {json-observationschema-schema-swe} using a JSON Schema validator.
step::: Validate that the SWE Common encoding is set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`JSONEncoding`].
step::: Validate the schema using test xref:/conf/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping[style=id%]
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping
target:: /req/swecommon-json/obsschema-mapping

test-purpose:: Verify that the mandatory fields are present in the schema.

test-method::
step::: Scan the schema and validate that at least one Time data component is present
step::: Validate the definition field of the Time component is one of: +
        - pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/phenomenonTime`]
        - pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.opengis.net/def/property/OGC/0/SamplingTime`]
        - pass:c,q,a,m,p[`http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/resultTime`]
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-json/observation-encoding
target:: /req/swecommon-json/observation-encoding

test-purpose:: Validate that the JSON representation of pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] resources is valid.

test-method::
step::: For every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`DataStream`] that advertises support for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] format:
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/datastreams/{dsId}/observations`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the response using a SWE Common validator implementing the JSON encoding rules.
====

test-method::
step::: For every pass:c,q,a,m,p[`ControlStream`] that advertises support for the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`] format:
step:::: Issue an HTTP GET request at pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{api_root}/controlstreams/{csId}/commands`] with the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Accept`] header set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-json-mediatype}`].
step:::: Validate that a document was returned with a status code 200.
step:::: Validate the response using a SWE Common validator implementing the JSON encoding rules.
====



=== Conformance Class "SWE Common Text Encoding"

[conformance_class]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-text
target:: /req/swecommon-text
inherit:: link:++http://www.opengis.net/spec/SWE/3.0/conf/text-encoding-rules++[]
classification:: Target Type:Web API

conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/mediatype-read
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/mediatype-write
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/obsschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/obsschema-mapping
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/observation-encoding
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/cmdschema-schema
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/cmdschema-mapping
conformance-test:: /conf/swecommon-text/command-encoding
====

// Media Types

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-text/mediatype-read
target:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-read

test-purpose:: Verify that the server supports the SWE Common Text format on retrieval operations.

test-method::
step::: For at least one of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and/or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources endpoint:
step:::: Verify that the server advertises support for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] in the API definition for retrieval operations.
step:::: Request resources from the resources endpoint with media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`].
step:::: Verify that the server responds with HTTP code 200.
step:::: Verify that the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Content-Type`] header of the response is set to pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`].
====

[abstract_test]
====
[%metadata]
identifier:: /conf/swecommon-text/mediatype-write
target:: /req/swecommon-text/mediatype-write

test-purpose:: Verify that the server advertises support for the SWE Common Text format on transactional operations.

test-method::
step::: For at least one of the pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Observation`] and/or pass:c,q,a,m,p[`Command`] resources endpoint:
step:::: Verify that the server advertises support for media type pass:c,q,a,m,p[`{swe-text-mediatype}`] in the API definition for CREATE or REPLACE operations.
====
